block Dragonspyre_Main_Quest_Tree {

############################
###      Settings        ###
############################

block Main_Account_Teleport_P2 {
	p1 friendtp CHANGENAME					# Change to the name of the account on client P2 (If you're not using 2 clients, just leave as-is)
}
block Main_Account_Teleport_P3 {
	p1 friendtp CHANGENAME					# Change to the name of the account on client P3 (If you're not using 3 clients, just leave as-is)
}
block Main_Account_Teleport_P4 {
	p1 friendtp CHANGENAME					# Change to the name of the account on client P4 (If you're not using 4 clients, just leave as-is)
}
block SpeedDelay {
	sleep 1							# Change "1" to however fast you'll like the bot to run in seconds. Between ( .1 - 2 ) is best for most 
}

block X_Interact_MASS {
	sleep 3
	times 20 {
		sendkey X, .1
	}
	sleep 2
}
block ZoneChange_MASS {
	waitforzonechange completion
	sleep 2
}
block Waiting_All_Arrival_Message {
	print "Waiting for all clients to reach this sigil."
}
block Combat_Dungeon_Complete_MASS {
	waitforcombat completion
	times 4 {
		entitytp Health
		entitytp Mana
	}
}
block Dungeon_X_Key_SOLO {
	sleep 3
	sendkey X, .5
}
block Dungeon_X_Key_MASS {
	sleep 5
	until windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground'] {
		sleep .1
	}
	sleep 1
	sendkey X, .5
}
block Exit_Quest_Menu {
	times 10 {
	clickwindow ['WorldView', 'NPCServicesWin', 'wndDialogMain', 'Exit']
	}
}
block Main_Is_Busy_On_Off {
	if p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'wndFriendsList'] {
		until NOT p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'wndFriendsList'] {
			p1 sendkey F, .25
			sleep .5
		}
	} else {
		until p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'wndFriendsList'] {
			p1 sendkey F, .25
			sleep .5
		}
	}
}
block Teleport_Main_To_Help_P1 {
	sleep .1
}
block Teleport_Main_To_Help_P2 {
	while p1 loading {
		sleep 2
	}
	sleep 1
	if NOT p1 loading {
		if NOT p1 incombat {
			until NOT p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'wndFriendsList'] {
				sleep .1
			}
			if NOT p1:p2 sameplace {
				if NOT p1:p2 samezone {
					call Main_Account_Teleport_P2
					sleep 2
				} else {
					call Main_Account_Teleport_P2
					while NOT p1:p2 sameplace {
						sleep 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
block Teleport_Main_To_Help_P3 {
	while p1 loading {
		sleep 2
	}
	sleep 1
	if NOT p1 loading {
		if NOT p1 incombat {
			until NOT p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'wndFriendsList'] {
				sleep .1
			}
			if NOT p1:p3 sameplace {
				if NOT p1:p3 samezone {
					call Main_Account_Teleport_P3
					sleep 2
				} else {
					call Main_Account_Teleport_P3
					while NOT p1:p3 sameplace {
						sleep 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}		
block Teleport_Main_To_Help_P4 {
	while p1 loading {
		sleep 2
	}
	sleep 1
	if NOT p1 loading {
		if NOT p1 incombat {
			until NOT p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'wndFriendsList'] {
				sleep .1
			}
			if NOT p1:p4 sameplace {
				if NOT p1:p4 samezone {
					call Main_Account_Teleport_P4
					sleep 2
				} else {
					call Main_Account_Teleport_P4
					while NOT p1:p4 sameplace {
						sleep 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
############################
### P1 Functional Blocks ###
############################
block Quest_Follow_Checker_P1 {
	if NOT p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow'] {
		while NOT p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'QuestLogMainlineAndSideButton'] {
			p1 sendkey Q, .5
			sleep 1
		}
		times 2 {
			p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'QuestLogMainlineAndSideButton']
		}
		if p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo0', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'LeftMainline'] {
			times 2 {
				p1 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo0']
			}
		} else {
			print "Unable to select main quest. Please select main quest."
		}
		while p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'QuestLogMainlineAndSideButton'] {
			p1 sendkey Q, .5
			sleep 1
		}
	}
}
block X_Interact_P1 {
	until p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground'] {
		sleep .1
	}
	call SpeedDelay
	times 10 {
		p1 sendkey X, .1
	}
}
block Dialogue_Complete_P1 {
	sleep 2
	while p1 hasdialogue {
		call SpeedDelay
		call Exit_Quest_Menu
	}
	call Exit_Quest_Menu
}
block ZoneChange_P1 {
	p1 waitforzonechange completion
	sleep 2
}
block Combat_Complete_P1 {
	p1 waitforcombat completion
	times 2 {
		p1 entitytp Health
		sleep .2
		p1 entitytp Mana
		sleep .2
	}
}

### P2 Functional Blocks ###

block Quest_Follow_Checker_P2 {
	if NOT p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow'] {
		while NOT p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'QuestLogMainlineAndSideButton'] {
			p2 sendkey Q, .5
			sleep 1
		}
		times 2 {
			p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'QuestLogMainlineAndSideButton']
		}
		if p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo0', 'questInfoWindow', 'wndQuestInfo', 'LeftMainline'] {
			times 2 {
				p2 clickwindow ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'wndQuestInfo0']
			}
		} else {
			print "Unable to select main quest. Please select main quest."
		}
		while p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'DeckConfiguration', 'wndQuestList', 'QuestLogMainlineAndSideButton'] {
			p2 sendkey Q, .5
			sleep 1
		}
	}
}
block X_Interact_P2 {
	until p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground'] {
		sleep .1
	}
	call SpeedDelay
	times 10 {
		p2 sendkey X, .1
	}
}
block Dialogue_Complete_P2 {
	sleep 2
	while p2 hasdialogue {
		call SpeedDelay
		call Exit_Quest_Menu
	}
	call Exit_Quest_Menu
}
block ZoneChange_P2 {
	p2 waitforzonechange completion
	sleep 2
}
block Combat_Complete_P2 {
	p2 waitforcombat completion
	times 2 {
		p2 entitytp Health
		sleep .2
		p2 entitytp Mana
		sleep .2
	}
	until NOT p1 windowdisabled ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'compassAndTeleporterButtons', 'GoHomeButton'] {
		sleep .1
	}
	p1 sendkey END, .2
	p1 sendkey END, .2
	p1 sendkey END, .2
}

block P1QuestsHandler {
	if p1 tracking_quest "bad blood" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to cyrus drake in the basilica" {
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "crystal-matic" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "go to portal to the atheneum in the basilica" {
			p1 tp quest
			sleep 2
			p1 tp quest
			sleep 3
			while p1 inzone DragonSpire/DS_Hub_Cathedral {
				p1 sendkey X, .1
				sleep 1
				if p1 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'btnSpellbook'] {
					break
				}
			}
			sleep 2
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to milos bookwyrm in the atheneum" {
			p1 tp XYZ(1837.1185302734375, 5725.92529296875, 533.4845581054688)
			call ZoneChange_P1
			p1 tp XYZ(-316.8902282714844, 2018.511474609375, -3.0517578125e-05)
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "holger's crystals" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "use knowledge crystal in the atheneum" {
			p1 tp XYZ(-136.2667694091797, 1003.4959716796875, 0.0001220703125)
			p1 turncam 4.40
			call X_Interact_P1
			sleep 2
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to milos bookwyrm in the atheneum" {
			p1 tp XYZ(-322.0686340332031, 2023.0235595703125, -3.0517578125e-05)
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "stone tower pilot" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "go to plaza in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp XYZ(6.752382278442383, 3360.91259765625, -3.0517578125e-05)
			call ZoneChange_P1
			p1 tp XYZ(6410.8427734375, 7071.33935546875, 383.11700439453125)
			call ZoneChange_P1
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to zarathax in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "the crystal towers" {
		while p1 tracking_goal "defeat loathsome creeper and collect tower key in the tower archives" {
			until p1 incombat {
				p1 tp quest
				sleep .1
			}
			call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P1
			call Combat_Complete_P1
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to zarathax in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "the towering crystal" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "go to first tower in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			sleep 2
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "use yellow crystal in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp XYZ(17959.501953125, -10640.263671875, 1529.189208984375)
			call X_Interact_P1
			sleep 2
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to zarathax in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "friend of my friend" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to lidia brightmoon in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp XYZ(15009.537109375, 11387.5234375, 180.00460815429688)
			call ZoneChange_P1
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
		while p1 tracking_goal "defeat soldiers in the tower archives" {
			if p1 inzone DragonSpire/DS_A1_Knowledge/Interiors/DS_ToK_T3 {
				p1 tp XYZ(20.322731018066406, 1375.295654296875, 0.123504638671875)
				call ZoneChange_P1
			}
			until p1 incombat {
				p1 tp XYZ(9622.0693359375, 13455.6591796875, 30.012664794921875)
				call SpeedDelay
				p1 entitytp Warrior-Female-Horns-Black-R7
				call SpeedDelay
			}
			call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P1
			call Combat_Complete_P1
			p1 tp XYZ(9622.0693359375, 13455.6591796875, 30.012664794921875)
		} 
		if p1 tracking_goal "go to desk in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp XYZ(850.8002319335938, -503.90557861328125, 0.123504638671875)
			sleep 2
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to zarathax in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp XYZ(26.67760467529297, 1381.3082275390625, 0.12347412109375)
			call ZoneChange_P1
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "key to trust" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "go to second tower in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			sleep 2
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "use orange crystal in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp XYZ(3659.364013671875, -8108.404296875, 1503.974609375)
			call X_Interact_P1
			sleep 2
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to zarathax in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "wizard and crystal" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "defeat petrov gloomstrider in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P1
			if playercount 1 {
				call Dungeon_X_Key_SOLO
				call ZoneChange_P1
				p1 tp quest
				call Combat_Complete_P1
				p1 tp XYZ(28.310998916625977, 1348.941650390625, -1.1190185546875)
				call ZoneChange_P1
			} else {
				if sameplace {
					if windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground'] {
						if windowtext ["WorldView", "NPCRangeWin", "wndTitleBackground", "NPCRangeTxtTitle"] "petrov's tower" {
							call Dungeon_X_Key_MASS
							call ZoneChange_MASS
							tp quest
							call Combat_Dungeon_Complete_MASS
							tp XYZ(28.310998916625977, 1348.941650390625, -1.1190185546875)
							call ZoneChange_MASS
						}
					}
				} else {
					call Waiting_All_Arrival_Message
				}
			}
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to zarathax in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "the last key" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "go to third tower in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			sleep 2
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "use green crystal in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp XYZ(9527.98828125, -2234.9873046875, 1511.8875732421875)
			call X_Interact_P1
			sleep 2
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to zarathax in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "the dragon's claw" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "find dragon claw key in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			sleep 2
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to zarathax in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "portal combat" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "go to tower in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			sleep 2
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "use blue crystal in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp XYZ(12140.0, -16524.0, 1828.0)
			call X_Interact_P1
			sleep 2
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "go to wizard's tower in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			sleep 2
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "defeat zarathax in the tower archives" {
			p1 tp quest
			call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P1
			if playercount 1 {
				call Dungeon_X_Key_SOLO
				call ZoneChange_P1
				p1 tp quest
				call Combat_Complete_P1
				p1 tp XYZ(4.759757041931152, 1381.898681640625, 0.123504638671875)
				call ZoneChange_P1
			} else {
				if sameplace {
					if windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground'] {
						if windowtext ["WorldView", "NPCRangeWin", "wndTitleBackground", "NPCRangeTxtTitle"] "zarathax's tower" {
							call Dungeon_X_Key_MASS
							call ZoneChange_MASS
							tp quest
							call Combat_Dungeon_Complete_MASS
							tp XYZ(4.759757041931152, 1381.898681640625, 0.123504638671875)
							call ZoneChange_MASS
						}
					}
				} else {
					call Waiting_All_Arrival_Message
				}
			}
			return
		}
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to milos bookwyrm in the atheneum" {
			p1 tp XYZ(-32141.669921875, -13975.9853515625, 30.00592041015625)
			call ZoneChange_P1
			p1 tp XYZ(1868.481689453125, 5727.2587890625, 533.4906005859375)
			call ZoneChange_P1
			p1 tp XYZ(-338.41583251953125, 2009.6201171875, 0.000152587890625)
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "warkeeper" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to laszlo starblade in plaza of conquests" {
			p1 tp XYZ(-3.995976209640503, 3358.250732421875, -9.1552734375e-05)
			call ZoneChange_P1
			p1 tp XYZ(465.9552307128906, 11264.3740234375, 791.10546875)
			call ZoneChange_P1
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "general recommendation" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "talk to zanna fireflower in plaza of conquests" {
			p1 tp quest
			call X_Interact_P1
			call Dialogue_Complete_P1
			return
		}
	}
	if p1 tracking_quest "battle on" {
		if p1 tracking_goal "defeat sysiphan in plaza of conquests" {
			p1 tp XYZ(7593.8154296875, -4017.114501953125, 180.00466918945312)
			call Teleport_Main_To_Help_P1
			if playercount 1 {
				call Dungeon_X_Key_SOLO
				call ZoneChange_P1
				p1 tp quest
				call Combat_Complete_P1
				p1 tp XYZ(12.216130256652832, 1347.194580078125, -1.1190185546875)
				call ZoneChange_P1

	loop {
		if playercount 1 {
			if p1 loading {
				sleep .01
			} else {
				break
			}
		} elif playercount 2 {
			if p1 loading {
				sleep .01
			} elif p2 loading {
				sleep .01
			} else {
				break
			}
		} elif playercount 3 {
			if p1 loading {
				sleep .01
			} elif p2 loading {
				sleep .01
			} elif p3 loading {
				sleep .01
			} else {
				break
			}
		} elif playercount 4 {
			if p1 loading {
				sleep .01
			} elif p2 loading {
				sleep .01
			} elif p3 loading {
				sleep .01
			} elif p4 loading {
				sleep .01
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
	}
	if playercount 1 {
		call P1QuestsHandler
		call Quest_Follow_Checker_P1
	} elif playercount 2 {
		call P2QuestsHandler
		call Quest_Follow_Checker_P2
	} elif playercount 3 {
		call P2QuestsHandler
		call Quest_Follow_Checker_P2
		call P3QuestsHandler
		call Quest_Follow_Checker_P3
	} elif playercount 4 {
		call P2QuestsHandler
		call Quest_Follow_Checker_P2
		call P3QuestsHandler
		call Quest_Follow_Checker_P3
		call P4QuestsHandler
		call Quest_Follow_Checker_P4
	}
} #Ends the entire block of Auto Quester
